+function Initialize-StepWatchdog {
+  # Start suspended and assign the job before any child code can spawn a process. Windows keeps descendants in
+  # the job even after their parent exits and accounts for CPU used by children that exit between polls.
+  Add-Type -TypeDefinition @'
+using System;
+using System.ComponentModel;
+using System.Diagnostics;
+using System.IO;
+using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
+using System.Text;
+using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
+using System.Threading;
+
+public sealed class RunnerStep : IDisposable {
+  [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
+  struct Startup {
+    public int size;
+    public IntPtr reserved, desktop, title;
+    public int x, y, width, height, xChars, yChars, fill, flags;
+    public short show, reservedSize;
+    public IntPtr reservedBytes, input, output, error;
+  }
+  [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
+  struct ProcessInfo {
+    public IntPtr process, thread;
+    public int pid, tid;
+  }
+  [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
+  public struct Usage {
+    public long UserTime, KernelTime, PeriodUserTime, PeriodKernelTime;
+    public uint PageFaults, Total, Active, Terminated;
+    public long CpuTime { get { return UserTime + KernelTime; } }
+  }
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern IntPtr CreateJobObject(IntPtr attrs, IntPtr name);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool SetInformationJobObject(IntPtr job, int kind, byte[] data, int size);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool QueryInformationJobObject(IntPtr job, int kind, out Usage data, int size, IntPtr written);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool AssignProcessToJobObject(IntPtr job, IntPtr process);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool TerminateJobObject(IntPtr job, uint code);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool TerminateProcess(IntPtr process, uint code);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern uint ResumeThread(IntPtr thread);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool SetHandleInformation(IntPtr handle, uint mask, uint flags);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll")] static extern IntPtr GetStdHandle(int number);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll")] static extern IntPtr GetCurrentProcess();
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool DuplicateHandle(IntPtr source, IntPtr handle, IntPtr target, out IntPtr copy, uint access, bool inherit, uint options);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll")] static extern bool CloseHandle(IntPtr handle);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Unicode, SetLastError = true)]
+  static extern bool CreateProcess(string exe, StringBuilder command, IntPtr processAttrs, IntPtr threadAttrs,
+    bool inherit, uint flags, IntPtr env, string cwd, ref Startup startup, out ProcessInfo info);
+
+  readonly IntPtr job;
+  public Process Root { get; private set; }
+  public RunnerStep(bool gpu) {
+    job = CreateJobObject(IntPtr.Zero, IntPtr.Zero);
+    if (job == IntPtr.Zero) throw new Win32Exception();
+    if (!gpu) return; // A successful ordinary queue launcher may leave background work running.
+    // JOBOBJECT_EXTENDED_LIMIT_INFORMATION, LimitFlags = JOB_OBJECT_LIMIT_KILL_ON_JOB_CLOSE.
+    byte[] limits = new byte[IntPtr.Size == 8 ? 144 : 112];
+    limits[17] = 0x20;
+    if (!SetInformationJobObject(job, 9, limits, limits.Length)) {
+      var error = new Win32Exception();
+      CloseHandle(job);
+      throw error;
+    }
+  }
+  public void Start(string exe, string args, string cwd, string output, string error) {
+    using (var stdout = new FileStream(output, FileMode.CreateNew, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.ReadWrite))
+    using (var stderr = new FileStream(error, FileMode.CreateNew, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.ReadWrite)) {
+      var startup = new Startup { size = Marshal.SizeOf(typeof(Startup)), flags = 0x100 }; // STARTF_USESTDHANDLES
+      startup.output = stdout.SafeFileHandle.DangerousGetHandle();
+      startup.error = stderr.SafeFileHandle.DangerousGetHandle();
+      if (!SetHandleInformation(startup.output, 1, 1) || !SetHandleInformation(startup.error, 1, 1))
+        throw new Win32Exception();
+      // A caller may have no stdin (for example, the queue running under a service).
+      IntPtr self = GetCurrentProcess();
+      DuplicateHandle(self, GetStdHandle(-10), self, out startup.input, 0, true, 2);
+      ProcessInfo info;
+      try {
+        var command = new StringBuilder("\"" + exe + "\" " + args);
+        if (!CreateProcess(exe, command, IntPtr.Zero, IntPtr.Zero, true, 4, IntPtr.Zero, cwd, ref startup, out info))
+          throw new Win32Exception();
+      } finally {
+        if (startup.input != IntPtr.Zero) CloseHandle(startup.input);
+      }
+      bool started = false;
+      try {
+        if (!AssignProcessToJobObject(job, info.process)) throw new Win32Exception();
+        Root = Process.GetProcessById(info.pid);
+        var handle = Root.Handle; // Keep the process identity and its exit code after exit.
+        if (ResumeThread(info.thread) == UInt32.MaxValue) throw new Win32Exception();
+        started = true;
+      } finally {
+        if (!started) TerminateProcess(info.process, 125);
+        CloseHandle(info.thread);
+        CloseHandle(info.process);
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  public Usage ReadUsage() {
+    Usage usage;
+    if (!QueryInformationJobObject(job, 1, out usage, Marshal.SizeOf(typeof(Usage)), IntPtr.Zero))
+      throw new Win32Exception();
+    return usage;
+  }
+  public void Kill() {
+    if (!TerminateJobObject(job, 124)) throw new Win32Exception();
+  }
+  public void Dispose() {
+    if (Root != null) Root.Dispose();
+    CloseHandle(job);
+  }
+}
+
+public sealed class RunnerStepFiles : IDisposable {
+  readonly FileSystemWatcher watcher;
+  readonly string folder;
+  int changes;
+  public RunnerStepFiles(string output) {
+    if (String.IsNullOrEmpty(output)) return;
+    folder = Path.GetFullPath(output).TrimEnd('\\', '/') + Path.DirectorySeparatorChar;
+    string parent = folder;
+    while (!Directory.Exists(parent)) parent = Path.GetDirectoryName(parent.TrimEnd('\\', '/'));
+    watcher = new FileSystemWatcher(parent) { IncludeSubdirectories = true, NotifyFilter = NotifyFilters.FileName };
+    watcher.Created += Changed;
+    watcher.Renamed += Changed;
+    watcher.Error += (sender, e) => Interlocked.Increment(ref changes); // An overflow also means file activity.
+    watcher.EnableRaisingEvents = true;
+  }
+  void Changed(object sender, FileSystemEventArgs e) {
+    if (e.FullPath.StartsWith(folder, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) Interlocked.Increment(ref changes);
+  }
+  public int Changes { get { return Volatile.Read(ref changes); } }
+  public void Dispose() { if (watcher != null) watcher.Dispose(); }
+}
+
+public sealed class RunnerStepOutput : IDisposable {
+  // One shared list for Godot, Blender and other steps. Keep the matched phrase in the log reason.
+  static readonly Regex fatal = new Regex("Jolt Physics job system exceeded|Out of memory|Assertion failed", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
+  static readonly Regex errorLine = new Regex(@"\b(error|fatal|exception|failed|failure|assertion)\b", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
+  readonly FileStream source;
+  readonly Stream target;
+  readonly byte[] bytes = new byte[65536];
+  readonly char[] chars = new char[65536];
+  readonly Decoder decoder = Encoding.UTF8.GetDecoder();
+  readonly StringBuilder line = new StringBuilder();
+  string previousError;
+  int repeats;
+  bool longLine;
+  public string KnownFatal { get; private set; }
+  public bool RepeatedError { get; private set; }
+  public bool Pending { get { return source.Position < source.Length; } }
+  public RunnerStepOutput(string path, bool stderr) {
+    source = new FileStream(path, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read, FileShare.ReadWrite);
+    target = stderr ? Console.OpenStandardError() : Console.OpenStandardOutput();
+  }
+  public void ResetRepeats() { previousError = null; repeats = 0; RepeatedError = false; }
+  public bool Drain(bool scan) {
+    int count = source.Read(bytes, 0, bytes.Length);
+    if (count == 0) return false;
+    target.Write(bytes, 0, count);
+    target.Flush();
+    if (!scan) return true;
+    int length = decoder.GetChars(bytes, 0, count, chars, 0);
+    for (int i = 0; i < length; i++) {
+      if (chars[i] == '\n') {
+        string text = line.ToString().Trim();
+        ScanFatal(text);
+        if (longLine || !errorLine.IsMatch(text)) { previousError = null; repeats = 0; }
+        else {
+          repeats = text == previousError ? repeats + 1 : 1;
+          previousError = text;
+          if (repeats >= 50) RepeatedError = true;
+        }
+        line.Clear();
+        longLine = false;
+      } else if (line.Length == 8192) {
+        ScanFatal(line.ToString());
+        line.Remove(0, 4096);
+        longLine = true;
+        line.Append(chars[i]);
+      } else line.Append(chars[i]);
+    }
+    ScanFatal(line.ToString());
+    return true;
+  }
+  void ScanFatal(string text) {
+    if (KnownFatal != null) return;
+    var match = fatal.Match(text);
+    if (match.Success) KnownFatal = match.Value;
+  }
+  public void Dispose() { source.Dispose(); }
+}
+'@
+}
+function Get-StepLimit($Value, $Default, $Name) {
+  $number = $Default
+  if ($Value -and ($Value -notmatch '^\d+$' -or -not [int]::TryParse($Value, [ref]$number) -or $number -lt 1)) {
+    throw "$Name must be a positive number of seconds"
+  }
+  return $number
+}
+function Stop-StepTree($Job, $Log, $Step) {
+  # Keep the claim while Windows still reports active processes, including during cleanup after a watchdog error.
+  while (($usage = $Job.ReadUsage()).Active) {
+    try { $Job.Kill() }
+    catch { Write-KillLog $Log "$Step kill failed, retrying with GPU claim held: $($_.Exception.Message)" }
+    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 50
+  }
+  Write-KillLog $Log "$Step kill verified: all $($usage.Total) processes gone (root PID $($Job.Root.Id))"
+}

+  $job = $files = $stdout = $stderr = $null
+  $keepSpool = $true
+  $finished = $false
+  try {
+    $cwd = (Get-Location).Path
+    $target = if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Exe -PathType Leaf) { (Get-Item -LiteralPath $Exe).FullName }
+              else { (Get-Command -Name $Exe -CommandType Application | Select-Object -First 1).Source }
+    $arguments = $argLine
+    if ($target -match '\.(bat|cmd)$') {
+      $arguments = '/d /s /c ""' + $target + '" ' + $argLine + '"'
+      $target = $env:ComSpec
+    }
+    if ($stepOut) { $stepOut = $ExecutionContext.SessionState.Path.GetUnresolvedProviderPathFromPSPath($stepOut) }
+    $files = New-Object RunnerStepFiles($stepOut)
+    $job = New-Object RunnerStep([bool]$gpuToken)
+    $null = [IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($spool)
+    $timer = [Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
+    $job.Start($target, $arguments, $cwd, $outPath, $errPath)
+    $canReleaseGpu = $false
+  } catch {
+    # No child code runs unless Start attached it to the job and resumed it.
+    if ($job -and $job.Root) { Stop-StepTree $job $log $stepName }
+    if ($job) { $job.Dispose() }
+    if ($files) { $files.Dispose() }
+    Write-KillLog $log "START FAILED $stepName ($Exe): $($_.Exception.Message)"
+    exit 125
+  }
+  try {
+    $stdout = New-Object RunnerStepOutput($outPath, $false)
+    $stderr = New-Object RunnerStepOutput($errPath, $true)
+    $lastProgress = 0.0
+    $lastCpu = 0
+    $lastFiles = 0
+    while ($true) {
+      $readOut = $stdout.Drain($true)
+      $readErr = $stderr.Drain($true)
+      $fatal = if ($stdout.KnownFatal) { $stdout.KnownFatal } else { $stderr.KnownFatal }
+      # The two streams have separate handles. Any output on the other stream breaks a repeat streak.
+      if ($readOut) { $stderr.ResetRepeats() }
+      if ($readErr) { $stdout.ResetRepeats() }
+      $elapsed = $timer.Elapsed.TotalSeconds
+      $usage = $job.ReadUsage()
+      $fileCount = $files.Changes
+      if ($readOut -or $readErr -or $usage.CpuTime -gt $lastCpu -or $fileCount -ne $lastFiles) {
+        $lastProgress = $elapsed
+      }
+      $lastCpu = $usage.CpuTime
+      $lastFiles = $fileCount
+      $pendingOutput = $stdout.Pending -or $stderr.Pending
+      if ($fatal) { $reason = "FATAL $fatal"; break }
+      if ($stdout.RepeatedError -or $stderr.RepeatedError) { $reason = 'FATAL repeated error line 50 times'; break }
+      if ($soloMax -and $claimTimer.Elapsed.TotalSeconds -ge $soloMax) { $reason = "SOLO CAP after $soloMax s"; break }
+      if ($job.Root.HasExited -and (-not $gpuToken -or -not $usage.Active) -and -not $pendingOutput) {
+        $finished = $true
+        $keepSpool = [bool]$usage.Active
+        if ($keepSpool) { Write-Output "run_timeout: background output continues in $spool" }
+        exit $job.Root.ExitCode
+      }
+      if ($elapsed -ge $Seconds) { $reason = 'CEILING'; break }
+      if ($elapsed - $lastProgress -ge $idleSeconds) { $reason = "IDLE after $idleSeconds s without output, new files or CPU progress"; break }
+      if (-not $pendingOutput) {
+        $waitMs = [Math]::Min(250, ($Seconds - $elapsed) * 1000)
+        $waitMs = [Math]::Min($waitMs, ($idleSeconds - ($elapsed - $lastProgress)) * 1000)
+        if ($soloMax) { $waitMs = [Math]::Min($waitMs, ($soloMax - $claimTimer.Elapsed.TotalSeconds) * 1000) }
+        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds ([int][Math]::Max(1, $waitMs))
+      }
+    }
+    $stoppedAfter = [Math]::Round($elapsed, 3)
+    Stop-StepTree $job $log $stepName
+    $canReleaseGpu = $true
+    $finished = $true
+    # Descendants have closed their streams. Copy the last bytes before reporting the stop.
+    while ($stdout.Drain($false)) { }
+    while ($stderr.Drain($false)) { }
+    Write-KillLog $log "TIMEOUT $stepName ($Exe): $reason at $stoppedAfter s (ceiling $Seconds s). Child logs: $spool"
+    exit 124
+  } finally {
+    if (-not $finished) { Stop-StepTree $job $log $stepName }
+    $canReleaseGpu = $true
+    if ($stdout) { $stdout.Dispose() }
+    if ($stderr) { $stderr.Dispose() }
+    $files.Dispose()
+    $job.Dispose()
+    if (-not $keepSpool) {
+      # Only delete these two files and the empty scratch directory we created.
+      try {
+        [IO.File]::Delete($outPath)
+        [IO.File]::Delete($errPath)
+        [IO.Directory]::Delete($spool)
+      } catch { Write-Output "run_timeout: child log cleanup failed in $spool : $($_.Exception.Message)" }
+    }
+  }
+}
+# Outer agents keep their original separate logs and repo/commit progress checks.

// Issue 946: under --fast a row runs when it is marked fast or it is the gate.
function fastSelected(checks) {
  return checks.filter(c => c.fast || c.gate);
}

// ---------- running ----------

function printRow(row) {
  console.log(row.result + ': ' + row.check + ' (' + row.seconds.toFixed(2) + ' s) | ' + row.detail);
  if (row.result === 'FAIL') console.log('\nFAIL ' + row.check + ' (first 15 output lines):\n' + row.output.split(/\r?\n/).slice(0, 15).join('\n'));
}

async function execute(c, baseline) {
  const start = Date.now();
  const row = { check: c.name, result: 'FAIL', seconds: 0, detail: '', output: '' };
  let temp;
  try {
    const original = path.join(ROOT, c.file);
    const source = fs.readFileSync(original, 'utf8');
    let childArgs = [original, ...c.args];
    const compact = source.trim().replace(/\s/g, '');
    if (compact.length && compact.length % 4 === 0 && /^[A-Za-z0-9+/]+={0,2}$/.test(compact)) {
      const decoded = Buffer.from(compact, 'base64');
      if (decoded.toString('base64') !== compact) throw new Error('Invalid base64');
      temp = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(__dirname, '.checks-temp-'));
      const target = path.join(temp, 'decoded.js');
      fs.writeFileSync(target, decoded);
      // Compile the temporary source with the original module location so sibling
      // requires and __dirname keep working without changing the stored tool.
      const bootstrap = "const fs=require('fs'),M=require('module'),p=require('path');const f=process.argv[1],t=process.argv[2];process.argv=[process.execPath,f,...process.argv.slice(3)];const m=new M(f);m.filename=f;m.paths=M._nodeModulePaths(p.dirname(f));require.main=m;m._compile(fs.readFileSync(t,'utf8'),f);";
      childArgs = ['-e', bootstrap, original, target, ...c.args];
    }
    const run = await new Promise(resolve => {
      const child = spawn(process.execPath, childArgs, { cwd: ROOT, env: { ...process.env, VS_ROOT: ROOT }, windowsHide: true });
      let output = '', error, timedOut = false;
      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
        timedOut = true;
        if (process.platform === 'win32') {
          const killer = spawn('taskkill', ['/pid', String(child.pid), '/T', '/F'], { windowsHide: true, stdio: 'ignore' });
          killer.on('error', () => child.kill());
        } else child.kill('SIGKILL');
      }, c.timeout * 1000);
      child.stdout.on('data', b => { output += b; });
      child.stderr.on('data', b => { output += b; });
      child.on('error', e => { error = e.message; });
      child.on('close', code => { clearTimeout(timer); resolve({ output, code, error, timedOut }); });
    });
    row.output = ascii(run.output);
    row.exitCode = run.code;
    if (run.timedOut) { row.detail = 'FAIL: timed out after ' + c.timeout + ' s'; return row; }
    if (run.error) throw new Error(run.error);
    if (c.kind === 'optional' && run.code !== 0 && /ENOENT|Cannot find module|not recognised|Godot.*(?:not found|required|missing)/i.test(run.output)) {
      row.result = 'SKIP';
      row.detail = 'SKIP: ' + (run.output.split(/\r?\n/).find(l => /ENOENT|Cannot find module|not recognised|Godot/i.test(l)) || 'Missing dependency');
      return row;
    }
    let verdict;
    if (c.kind === 'naming') verdict = classifyNaming(run, baseline);
    else if (c.kind === 'pack') verdict = classifyPack(run, baseline);
    else if (c.kind === 'findings') verdict = classifyFindings(run, baseline);
    else if (c.kind === 'drift') verdict = classifyDrift(run, baseline);
    else verdict = classifyGeneric(run);
    row.result = verdict.result;
    row.detail = verdict.detail;
  } catch (e) {
    row.result = c.kind === 'optional' && e.code === 'ENOENT' ? 'SKIP' : 'FAIL';
    row.detail = (row.result === 'SKIP' ? 'SKIP: ' : 'FAIL: ') + e.message;
  } finally {
    if (temp) fs.rmSync(temp, { recursive: true, force: true });
    row.seconds = Math.round((Date.now() - start) / 10) / 100;
    row.detail = ascii(row.detail).replace(/[\r\n\t]+/g, ' ');
  }
  return row;
}

  const args = process.argv.slice(2);

+  }
+  if (mode === 'idle') {

+The PC watchdog caps each solo claim at 1200 seconds by default. A longer bench sets `PC_GPU_SOLO_MAX_S` for that
+step, and a job with several timed sessions claims the card once per session. Each wrapped step has a ceiling sized to

+function Initialize-StepWatchdog {
+  # Start suspended and assign the job before any child code can spawn a process. Windows keeps descendants in
+  # the job even after their parent exits and accounts for CPU used by children that exit between polls.
+  Add-Type -TypeDefinition @'
+using System;
+using System.ComponentModel;
+using System.Diagnostics;
+using System.IO;
+using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
+using System.Text;
+using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
+using System.Threading;
+
+public sealed class RunnerStep : IDisposable {
+  [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
+  struct Startup {
+    public int size;
+    public IntPtr reserved, desktop, title;
+    public int x, y, width, height, xChars, yChars, fill, flags;
+    public short show, reservedSize;
+    public IntPtr reservedBytes, input, output, error;
+  }
+  [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
+  struct ProcessInfo {
+    public IntPtr process, thread;
+    public int pid, tid;
+  }
+  [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
+  public struct Usage {
+    public long UserTime, KernelTime, PeriodUserTime, PeriodKernelTime;
+    public uint PageFaults, Total, Active, Terminated;
+    public long CpuTime { get { return UserTime + KernelTime; } }
+  }
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern IntPtr CreateJobObject(IntPtr attrs, IntPtr name);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool SetInformationJobObject(IntPtr job, int kind, byte[] data, int size);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool QueryInformationJobObject(IntPtr job, int kind, out Usage data, int size, IntPtr written);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool AssignProcessToJobObject(IntPtr job, IntPtr process);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool TerminateJobObject(IntPtr job, uint code);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool TerminateProcess(IntPtr process, uint code);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern uint ResumeThread(IntPtr thread);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool SetHandleInformation(IntPtr handle, uint mask, uint flags);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll")] static extern IntPtr GetStdHandle(int number);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll")] static extern IntPtr GetCurrentProcess();
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool DuplicateHandle(IntPtr source, IntPtr handle, IntPtr target, out IntPtr copy, uint access, bool inherit, uint options);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll")] static extern bool CloseHandle(IntPtr handle);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Unicode, SetLastError = true)]
+  static extern bool CreateProcess(string exe, StringBuilder command, IntPtr processAttrs, IntPtr threadAttrs,
+    bool inherit, uint flags, IntPtr env, string cwd, ref Startup startup, out ProcessInfo info);
+
+  readonly IntPtr job;
+  public Process Root { get; private set; }
+  public RunnerStep(bool gpu) {
+    job = CreateJobObject(IntPtr.Zero, IntPtr.Zero);
+    if (job == IntPtr.Zero) throw new Win32Exception();
+    if (!gpu) return; // A successful ordinary queue launcher may leave background work running.
+    // JOBOBJECT_EXTENDED_LIMIT_INFORMATION, LimitFlags = JOB_OBJECT_LIMIT_KILL_ON_JOB_CLOSE.
+    byte[] limits = new byte[IntPtr.Size == 8 ? 144 : 112];
+    limits[17] = 0x20;
+    if (!SetInformationJobObject(job, 9, limits, limits.Length)) {
+      var error = new Win32Exception();
+      CloseHandle(job);
+      throw error;
+    }
+  }
+  public void Start(string exe, string args, string cwd, string output, string error) {
+    using (var stdout = new FileStream(output, FileMode.CreateNew, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.ReadWrite))
+    using (var stderr = new FileStream(error, FileMode.CreateNew, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.ReadWrite)) {
+      var startup = new Startup { size = Marshal.SizeOf(typeof(Startup)), flags = 0x100 }; // STARTF_USESTDHANDLES
+      startup.output = stdout.SafeFileHandle.DangerousGetHandle();
+      startup.error = stderr.SafeFileHandle.DangerousGetHandle();
+      if (!SetHandleInformation(startup.output, 1, 1) || !SetHandleInformation(startup.error, 1, 1))
+        throw new Win32Exception();
+      // A caller may have no stdin (for example, the queue running under a service).
+      IntPtr self = GetCurrentProcess();
+      DuplicateHandle(self, GetStdHandle(-10), self, out startup.input, 0, true, 2);
+      ProcessInfo info;
+      try {
+        var command = new StringBuilder("\"" + exe + "\" " + args);
+        if (!CreateProcess(exe, command, IntPtr.Zero, IntPtr.Zero, true, 4, IntPtr.Zero, cwd, ref startup, out info))
+          throw new Win32Exception();
+      } finally {
+        if (startup.input != IntPtr.Zero) CloseHandle(startup.input);
+      }
+      bool started = false;
+      try {
+        if (!AssignProcessToJobObject(job, info.process)) throw new Win32Exception();
+        Root = Process.GetProcessById(info.pid);
+        var handle = Root.Handle; // Keep the process identity and its exit code after exit.
+        if (ResumeThread(info.thread) == UInt32.MaxValue) throw new Win32Exception();
+        started = true;
+      } finally {
+        if (!started) TerminateProcess(info.process, 125);
+        CloseHandle(info.thread);
+        CloseHandle(info.process);
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  public Usage ReadUsage() {
+    Usage usage;
+    if (!QueryInformationJobObject(job, 1, out usage, Marshal.SizeOf(typeof(Usage)), IntPtr.Zero))
+      throw new Win32Exception();
+    return usage;
+  }
+  public void Kill() {
+    if (!TerminateJobObject(job, 124)) throw new Win32Exception();
+  }
+  public void Dispose() {
+    if (Root != null) Root.Dispose();
+    CloseHandle(job);
+  }
+}
+
+public sealed class RunnerStepFiles : IDisposable {
+  readonly FileSystemWatcher watcher;
+  readonly string folder;
+  int changes;
+  public RunnerStepFiles(string output) {
+    if (String.IsNullOrEmpty(output)) return;
+    folder = Path.GetFullPath(output).TrimEnd('\\', '/') + Path.DirectorySeparatorChar;
+    string parent = folder;
+    while (!Directory.Exists(parent)) parent = Path.GetDirectoryName(parent.TrimEnd('\\', '/'));
+    watcher = new FileSystemWatcher(parent) { IncludeSubdirectories = true, NotifyFilter = NotifyFilters.FileName };
+    watcher.Created += Changed;
+    watcher.Renamed += Changed;
+    watcher.Error += (sender, e) => Interlocked.Increment(ref changes); // An overflow also means file activity.
+    watcher.EnableRaisingEvents = true;
+  }
+  void Changed(object sender, FileSystemEventArgs e) {
+    if (e.FullPath.StartsWith(folder, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) Interlocked.Increment(ref changes);
+  }
+  public int Changes { get { return Volatile.Read(ref changes); } }
+  public void Dispose() { if (watcher != null) watcher.Dispose(); }
+}
+
+public sealed class RunnerStepOutput : IDisposable {
+  // One shared list for Godot, Blender and other steps. Keep the matched phrase in the log reason.
+  static readonly Regex fatal = new Regex("Jolt Physics job system exceeded|Out of memory|Assertion failed", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
+  static readonly Regex errorLine = new Regex(@"\b(error|fatal|exception|failed|failure|assertion)\b", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
+  readonly FileStream source;
+  readonly Stream target;
+  readonly byte[] bytes = new byte[65536];
+  readonly char[] chars = new char[65536];
+  readonly Decoder decoder = Encoding.UTF8.GetDecoder();
+  readonly StringBuilder line = new StringBuilder();
+  string previousError;
+  int repeats;
+  bool longLine;
+  public string KnownFatal { get; private set; }
+  public bool RepeatedError { get; private set; }
+  public bool Pending { get { return source.Position < source.Length; } }
+  public RunnerStepOutput(string path, bool stderr) {
+    source = new FileStream(path, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read, FileShare.ReadWrite);
+    target = stderr ? Console.OpenStandardError() : Console.OpenStandardOutput();
+  }
+  public void ResetRepeats() { previousError = null; repeats = 0; RepeatedError = false; }
+  public bool Drain(bool scan) {
+    int count = source.Read(bytes, 0, bytes.Length);
+    if (count == 0) return false;
+    target.Write(bytes, 0, count);
+    target.Flush();
+    if (!scan) return true;
+    int length = decoder.GetChars(bytes, 0, count, chars, 0);
+    for (int i = 0; i < length; i++) {
+      if (chars[i] == '\n') {
+        string text = line.ToString().Trim();
+        ScanFatal(text);
+        if (longLine || !errorLine.IsMatch(text)) { previousError = null; repeats = 0; }
+        else {
+          repeats = text == previousError ? repeats + 1 : 1;
+          previousError = text;
+          if (repeats >= 50) RepeatedError = true;
+        }
+        line.Clear();
+        longLine = false;
+      } else if (line.Length == 8192) {
+        ScanFatal(line.ToString());
+        line.Remove(0, 4096);
+        longLine = true;
+        line.Append(chars[i]);
+      } else line.Append(chars[i]);
+    }
+    ScanFatal(line.ToString());
+    return true;
+  }
+  void ScanFatal(string text) {
+    if (KnownFatal != null) return;
+    var match = fatal.Match(text);
+    if (match.Success) KnownFatal = match.Value;
+  }
+  public void Dispose() { source.Dispose(); }
+}
+'@
+}
+function Get-StepLimit($Value, $Default, $Name) {
+  $number = $Default
+  if ($Value -and ($Value -notmatch '^\d+$' -or -not [int]::TryParse($Value, [ref]$number) -or $number -lt 1)) {
+    throw "$Name must be a positive number of seconds"
+  }
+  return $number
+}
+function Stop-StepTree($Job, $Log, $Step) {
+  # Keep the claim while Windows still reports active processes, including during cleanup after a watchdog error.
+  while (($usage = $Job.ReadUsage()).Active) {
+    try { $Job.Kill() }
+    catch { Write-KillLog $Log "$Step kill failed, retrying with GPU claim held: $($_.Exception.Message)" }
+    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 50
+  }
+  Write-KillLog $Log "$Step kill verified: all $($usage.Total) processes gone (root PID $($Job.Root.Id))"
+}

+  $errPath = Join-Path $spool 'stderr.log'
+  $job = $files = $stdout = $stderr = $null
+  $keepSpool = $true
+  $finished = $false
+  try {
+    $cwd = (Get-Location).Path
+    $target = if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Exe -PathType Leaf) { (Get-Item -LiteralPath $Exe).FullName }
+              else { (Get-Command -Name $Exe -CommandType Application | Select-Object -First 1).Source }
+    $arguments = $argLine
+    if ($target -match '\.(bat|cmd)$') {
+      $arguments = '/d /s /c ""' + $target + '" ' + $argLine + '"'
+      $target = $env:ComSpec
+    }
+    if ($stepOut) { $stepOut = $ExecutionContext.SessionState.Path.GetUnresolvedProviderPathFromPSPath($stepOut) }
+    $files = New-Object RunnerStepFiles($stepOut)
+    $job = New-Object RunnerStep([bool]$gpuToken)
+    $null = [IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($spool)
+    $timer = [Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
+    $job.Start($target, $arguments, $cwd, $outPath, $errPath)
+    $canReleaseGpu = $false
+  } catch {
+    # No child code runs unless Start attached it to the job and resumed it.
+    if ($job -and $job.Root) { Stop-StepTree $job $log $stepName }
+    if ($job) { $job.Dispose() }
+    if ($files) { $files.Dispose() }
+    Write-KillLog $log "START FAILED $stepName ($Exe): $($_.Exception.Message)"
+    exit 125
+  }
+  try {
+    $stdout = New-Object RunnerStepOutput($outPath, $false)
+    $stderr = New-Object RunnerStepOutput($errPath, $true)
+    $lastProgress = 0.0
+    $lastCpu = 0
+    $lastFiles = 0
+    while ($true) {
+      $readOut = $stdout.Drain($true)
+      $readErr = $stderr.Drain($true)
+      $fatal = if ($stdout.KnownFatal) { $stdout.KnownFatal } else { $stderr.KnownFatal }
+      # The two streams have separate handles. Any output on the other stream breaks a repeat streak.
+      if ($readOut) { $stderr.ResetRepeats() }
+      if ($readErr) { $stdout.ResetRepeats() }
+      $elapsed = $timer.Elapsed.TotalSeconds
+      $usage = $job.ReadUsage()
+      $fileCount = $files.Changes
+      if ($readOut -or $readErr -or $usage.CpuTime -gt $lastCpu -or $fileCount -ne $lastFiles) {
+        $lastProgress = $elapsed
+      }
+      $lastCpu = $usage.CpuTime
+      $lastFiles = $fileCount
+      $pendingOutput = $stdout.Pending -or $stderr.Pending
+      if ($fatal) { $reason = "FATAL $fatal"; break }
+      if ($stdout.RepeatedError -or $stderr.RepeatedError) { $reason = 'FATAL repeated error line 50 times'; break }
+      if ($soloMax -and $claimTimer.Elapsed.TotalSeconds -ge $soloMax) { $reason = "SOLO CAP after $soloMax s"; break }
+      if ($job.Root.HasExited -and (-not $gpuToken -or -not $usage.Active) -and -not $pendingOutput) {
+        $finished = $true
+        $keepSpool = [bool]$usage.Active
+        if ($keepSpool) { Write-Output "run_timeout: background output continues in $spool" }
+        exit $job.Root.ExitCode
+      }
+      if ($elapsed -ge $Seconds) { $reason = 'CEILING'; break }
+      if ($elapsed - $lastProgress -ge $idleSeconds) { $reason = "IDLE after $idleSeconds s without output, new files or CPU progress"; break }
+      if (-not $pendingOutput) {
+        $waitMs = [Math]::Min(250, ($Seconds - $elapsed) * 1000)
+        $waitMs = [Math]::Min($waitMs, ($idleSeconds - ($elapsed - $lastProgress)) * 1000)
+        if ($soloMax) { $waitMs = [Math]::Min($waitMs, ($soloMax - $claimTimer.Elapsed.TotalSeconds) * 1000) }
+        Start-Sleep -Milliseconds ([int][Math]::Max(1, $waitMs))
+      }
+    }
+    $stoppedAfter = [Math]::Round($elapsed, 3)
+    Stop-StepTree $job $log $stepName
+    $canReleaseGpu = $true
+    $finished = $true
+    # Descendants have closed their streams. Copy the last bytes before reporting the stop.
+    while ($stdout.Drain($false)) { }
+    while ($stderr.Drain($false)) { }
+    Write-KillLog $log "TIMEOUT $stepName ($Exe): $reason at $stoppedAfter s (ceiling $Seconds s). Child logs: $spool"
+    exit 124
+  } finally {
+    if (-not $finished) { Stop-StepTree $job $log $stepName }
+    $canReleaseGpu = $true
+    if ($stdout) { $stdout.Dispose() }
+    if ($stderr) { $stderr.Dispose() }
+    $files.Dispose()
+    $job.Dispose()
+    if (-not $keepSpool) {
+      # Only delete these two files and the empty scratch directory we created.
+      try {
+        [IO.File]::Delete($outPath)
+        [IO.File]::Delete($errPath)
+        [IO.Directory]::Delete($spool)
+      } catch { Write-Output "run_timeout: child log cleanup failed in $spool : $($_.Exception.Message)" }
+    }
+  }
+}

  static int Godot(string[] a) {
    string step = "other";
    int counter = 0;
    if (a.Any(x => x.IndexOf("--import", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)) step = "import";
    else if (a.Any(x => x.IndexOf("build_assets.gd", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)) { step = "build"; counter = Count("build"); }
    else if (a.Any(x => x.IndexOf("scene_gate.tscn", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) >= 0)) { step = "bench"; counter = Count("bench"); }
    string fail = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("FAKE_GODOT_FAIL") ?? "";
    if (fail == step || fail == step + counter) {
      Console.Error.WriteLine("fake godot: " + step + (counter > 0 ? counter.ToString() : "") + " failed");
      return FailCode();
    }
    if (step == "build") {
      if (Env("FAKE_GODOT_NO_V3", "") != "1") {
        string proj = Arg(a, "--path");
        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path.Combine(proj, "gen"));
        File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(proj, "gen", "v3.txt"), "fake v3\\n");
      }
    }
    if (step == "bench") {
      if ((Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("FAKE_GODOT_NO_OUT") ?? "") != "bench" + counter) {
        string outDir = Arg(a, "--out");
        Directory.CreateDirectory(outDir);
        if ((Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("FAKE_GODOT_EMPTY_OUT") ?? "") != "bench" + counter)
          File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(outDir, "scene_gate.json"), "{}\\n");
      }
    }
    return 0;
  }
}

+public static class FakeGodot {
+  static void StartChild(string exe, string role, string output) {
+    Process.Start(new ProcessStartInfo(exe, role + " \"" + output + "\"") {
+      UseShellExecute = false, CreateNoWindow = true
+    });
+  }

+    if (mode == "branch") {
+      StartChild(exe, "leaf", output);
+      Thread.Sleep(Timeout.Infinite);
+      return 0;
+    }
+    if (mode == "new-files" || mode == "cpu") {
+      var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
+      int file = 0;
+      while (clock.ElapsedMilliseconds < 1800) {
+        if (mode == "cpu") Thread.SpinWait(10000);
+        else {
+          File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(output, "work-" + file++), "progress");
+          Thread.Sleep(150);
+        }
+      }
+      return 7;
+    }
+    if (mode == "mixed") {
+      for (int i = 0; i < 60; i++) {
+        Console.Error.WriteLine("ERROR: fixture retry");
+        Console.WriteLine("other output");
+      }
+      return 7;
+    }
+    StartChild(exe, "branch", output);
+    if (!SpinWait.SpinUntil(() => File.Exists(Path.Combine(output, "pid-leaf")), 5000)) return 9;
+    string claim = Path.Combine(output, "gpu", "gpu_slot_1");
+    if (mode == "idle" || mode == "solo") {
+      if (!File.Exists(claim) || !File.ReadAllText(claim).Contains("1192")) return 8;
+      Console.WriteLine("fixture saw held GPU claim");
+    }
+    if (mode == "idle") Console.Error.Write("last output before the hang");
+    if (mode == "jolt") Console.Error.Write("Jolt Physics job system exceeded the maximum number of jobs");
+    if (mode == "repeat") for (int i = 0; i < 50; i++) Console.WriteLine("ERROR: fixture retry");
+    if (mode == "solo") {
+      Console.WriteLine("launcher exited while descendants were still running");
+      return 7;
+    }
+    Thread.Sleep(Timeout.Infinite);
+    return 0;
+  }

+The PC watchdog caps each solo claim at 1200 seconds by default. A longer bench sets `PC_GPU_SOLO_MAX_S` for that
+step, and a job with several timed sessions claims the card once per session. Each wrapped step has a ceiling sized to
+its own work and an idle check for console output, new files in its output folder, and CPU time across its tree. The
+idle limit is 300 seconds for Godot and 600 seconds for Blender and other tools. A step can raise it with `TMO_IDLE_S`.
+Known fatal lines and 50 consecutive copies of one error line stop the step immediately. A stop returns 124, keeps the
+child logs, and records the step and reason in `timeouts.log`. The watchdog verifies the tree is gone before releasing
+the GPU claim. GPU admission has its own clock, with a 1800-second default in the agent runners and 3600 in the shared
+ship runner. A Jolt stall in the issue 1078 probe held the GPU solo for almost an hour and queued work timed out behind
+it (Board, Nov 19, issue 1192).

 function Write-KillLog($Log, $Line) {
   Write-Output "run_timeout: $Line"
-  try { "$(Get-Date -Format s) $Line" | Out-File -LiteralPath $Log -Append -Encoding utf8 }
-  catch { Write-Output "run_timeout: could not append kill log: $($_.Exception.Message)" }
+  for ($attempt = 1; ; $attempt++) {
+    try { [IO.File]::AppendAllText($Log, "$(Get-Date -Format s) $Line`r`n"); return }
+    catch [IO.IOException] {
+      # Several watchdogs can report to the same queue log.
+      if ($attempt -ge 10) { Write-Output "run_timeout: could not append kill log: $($_.Exception.Message)"; return }
+      Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 25
+    }
+    catch { Write-Output "run_timeout: could not append kill log: $($_.Exception.Message)"; return }
+  }

+function Initialize-StepWatchdog {
+  # Start suspended and assign the job before any child code can spawn a process. Windows keeps descendants in
+  # the job even after their parent exits and accounts for CPU used by children that exit between polls.
+  Add-Type -TypeDefinition @'
+using System;
+using System.ComponentModel;
+using System.Diagnostics;
+using System.IO;
+using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
+using System.Text;
+using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
+using System.Threading;
+
+public sealed class RunnerStep : IDisposable {
+  [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
+  struct Startup {
+    public int size;
+    public IntPtr reserved, desktop, title;
+    public int x, y, width, height, xChars, yChars, fill, flags;
+    public short show, reservedSize;
+    public IntPtr reservedBytes, input, output, error;
+  }
+  [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
+  struct ProcessInfo {
+    public IntPtr process, thread;
+    public int pid, tid;
+  }
+  [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
+  public struct Usage {
+    public long UserTime, KernelTime, PeriodUserTime, PeriodKernelTime;
+    public uint PageFaults, Total, Active, Terminated;
+    public long CpuTime { get { return UserTime + KernelTime; } }
+  }
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern IntPtr CreateJobObject(IntPtr attrs, IntPtr name);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool SetInformationJobObject(IntPtr job, int kind, byte[] data, int size);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool QueryInformationJobObject(IntPtr job, int kind, out Usage data, int size, IntPtr written);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool AssignProcessToJobObject(IntPtr job, IntPtr process);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool TerminateJobObject(IntPtr job, uint code);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool TerminateProcess(IntPtr process, uint code);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern uint ResumeThread(IntPtr thread);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool SetHandleInformation(IntPtr handle, uint mask, uint flags);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll")] static extern IntPtr GetStdHandle(int number);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll")] static extern IntPtr GetCurrentProcess();
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool DuplicateHandle(IntPtr source, IntPtr handle, IntPtr target, out IntPtr copy, uint access, bool inherit, uint options);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll")] static extern bool CloseHandle(IntPtr handle);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Unicode, SetLastError = true)]
+  static extern bool CreateProcess(string exe, StringBuilder command, IntPtr processAttrs, IntPtr threadAttrs,
+    bool inherit, uint flags, IntPtr env, string cwd, ref Startup startup, out ProcessInfo info);
+
+  readonly IntPtr job;
+  public Process Root { get; private set; }
+  public RunnerStep(bool gpu) {
+    job = CreateJobObject(IntPtr.Zero, IntPtr.Zero);
+    if (job == IntPtr.Zero) throw new Win32Exception();
+    if (!gpu) return; // A successful ordinary queue launcher may leave background work running.
+    // JOBOBJECT_EXTENDED_LIMIT_INFORMATION, LimitFlags = JOB_OBJECT_LIMIT_KILL_ON_JOB_CLOSE.
+    byte[] limits = new byte[IntPtr.Size == 8 ? 144 : 112];
+    limits[17] = 0x20;
+    if (!SetInformationJobObject(job, 9, limits, limits.Length)) {
+      var error = new Win32Exception();
+      CloseHandle(job);
+      throw error;
+    }
+  }
+  public void Start(string exe, string args, string cwd, string output, string error) {
+    using (var stdout = new FileStream(output, FileMode.CreateNew, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.ReadWrite))
+    using (var stderr = new FileStream(error, FileMode.CreateNew, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.ReadWrite)) {
+      var startup = new Startup { size = Marshal.SizeOf(typeof(Startup)), flags = 0x100 }; // STARTF_USESTDHANDLES
+      startup.output = stdout.SafeFileHandle.DangerousGetHandle();
+      startup.error = stderr.SafeFileHandle.DangerousGetHandle();
+      if (!SetHandleInformation(startup.output, 1, 1) || !SetHandleInformation(startup.error, 1, 1))
+        throw new Win32Exception();
+      // A caller may have no stdin (for example, the queue running under a service).
+      IntPtr self = GetCurrentProcess();
+      DuplicateHandle(self, GetStdHandle(-10), self, out startup.input, 0, true, 2);
+      ProcessInfo info;
+      try {
+        var command = new StringBuilder("\"" + exe + "\" " + args);
+        if (!CreateProcess(exe, command, IntPtr.Zero, IntPtr.Zero, true, 4, IntPtr.Zero, cwd, ref startup, out info))
+          throw new Win32Exception();
+      } finally {
+        if (startup.input != IntPtr.Zero) CloseHandle(startup.input);
+      }
+      bool started = false;
+      try {
+        if (!AssignProcessToJobObject(job, info.process)) throw new Win32Exception();
+        Root = Process.GetProcessById(info.pid);
+        var handle = Root.Handle; // Keep the process identity and its exit code after exit.
+        if (ResumeThread(info.thread) == UInt32.MaxValue) throw new Win32Exception();
+        started = true;
+      } finally {
+        if (!started) TerminateProcess(info.process, 125);
+        CloseHandle(info.thread);
+        CloseHandle(info.process);
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  public Usage ReadUsage() {
+    Usage usage;
+    if (!QueryInformationJobObject(job, 1, out usage, Marshal.SizeOf(typeof(Usage)), IntPtr.Zero))
+      throw new Win32Exception();
+    return usage;
+  }
+  public void Kill() {
+    if (!TerminateJobObject(job, 124)) throw new Win32Exception();
+  }
+  public void Dispose() {
+    if (Root != null) Root.Dispose();
+    CloseHandle(job);
+  }
+}
+
+public sealed class RunnerStepFiles : IDisposable {
+  readonly FileSystemWatcher watcher;
+  readonly string folder;
+  int changes;
+  public RunnerStepFiles(string output) {
+    if (String.IsNullOrEmpty(output)) return;
+    folder = Path.GetFullPath(output).TrimEnd('\\', '/') + Path.DirectorySeparatorChar;
+    string parent = folder;
+    while (!Directory.Exists(parent)) parent = Path.GetDirectoryName(parent.TrimEnd('\\', '/'));
+    watcher = new FileSystemWatcher(parent) { IncludeSubdirectories = true, NotifyFilter = NotifyFilters.FileName };
+    watcher.Created += Changed;
+    watcher.Renamed += Changed;
+    watcher.Error += (sender, e) => Interlocked.Increment(ref changes); // An overflow also means file activity.
+    watcher.EnableRaisingEvents = true;
+  }
+  void Changed(object sender, FileSystemEventArgs e) {
+    if (e.FullPath.StartsWith(folder, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) Interlocked.Increment(ref changes);
+  }
+  public int Changes { get { return Volatile.Read(ref changes); } }
+  public void Dispose() { if (watcher != null) watcher.Dispose(); }
+}
+
+public sealed class RunnerStepOutput : IDisposable {
+  // One shared list for Godot, Blender and other steps. Keep the matched phrase in the log reason.
+  static readonly Regex fatal = new Regex("Jolt Physics job system exceeded|Out of memory|Assertion failed", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
+  static readonly Regex errorLine = new Regex(@"\b(error|fatal|exception|failed|failure|assertion)\b", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
+  readonly FileStream source;
+  readonly Stream target;
+  readonly byte[] bytes = new byte[65536];
+  readonly char[] chars = new char[65536];
+  readonly Decoder decoder = Encoding.UTF8.GetDecoder();
+  readonly StringBuilder line = new StringBuilder();
+  string previousError;
+  int repeats;
+  bool longLine;
+  public string KnownFatal { get; private set; }
+  public bool RepeatedError { get; private set; }
+  public bool Pending { get { return source.Position < source.Length; } }
+  public RunnerStepOutput(string path, bool stderr) {
+    source = new FileStream(path, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read, FileShare.ReadWrite);
+    target = stderr ? Console.OpenStandardError() : Console.OpenStandardOutput();
+  }
+  public void ResetRepeats() { previousError = null; repeats = 0; RepeatedError = false; }
+  public bool Drain(bool scan) {
+    int count = source.Read(bytes, 0, bytes.Length);
+    if (count == 0) return false;
+    target.Write(bytes, 0, count);
+    target.Flush();
+    if (!scan) return true;
+    int length = decoder.GetChars(bytes, 0, count, chars, 0);
+    for (int i = 0; i < length; i++) {
+      if (chars[i] == '\n') {
+        string text = line.ToString().Trim();
+        ScanFatal(text);
+        if (longLine || !errorLine.IsMatch(text)) { previousError = null; repeats = 0; }
+        else {
+          repeats = text == previousError ? repeats + 1 : 1;
+          previousError = text;
+          if (repeats >= 50) RepeatedError = true;
+        }
+        line.Clear();
+        longLine = false;
+      } else if (line.Length == 8192) {
+        ScanFatal(line.ToString());
+        line.Remove(0, 4096);
+        longLine = true;
+        line.Append(chars[i]);
+      } else line.Append(chars[i]);
+    }
+    ScanFatal(line.ToString());
+    return true;
+  }
+  void ScanFatal(string text) {
+    if (KnownFatal != null) return;
+    var match = fatal.Match(text);
+    if (match.Success) KnownFatal = match.Value;
+  }
+  public void Dispose() { source.Dispose(); }
+}
+'@
+}
+function Get-StepLimit($Value, $Default, $Name) {
+  $number = $Default
+  if ($Value -and ($Value -notmatch '^\d+$' -or -not [int]::TryParse($Value, [ref]$number) -or $number -lt 1)) {
+    throw "$Name must be a positive number of seconds"
+  }
+  return $number
+}
+function Stop-StepTree($Job, $Log, $Step) {
+  # Keep the claim while Windows still reports active processes, including during cleanup after a watchdog error.
+  while (($usage = $Job.ReadUsage()).Active) {
+    try { $Job.Kill() }
+    catch { Write-KillLog $Log "$Step kill failed, retrying with GPU claim held: $($_.Exception.Message)" }
+    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 50
+  }
+  Write-KillLog $Log "$Step kill verified: all $($usage.Total) processes gone (root PID $($Job.Root.Id))"

@@ -307,2 +307,12 @@ jobs and extend things out" (Ryan, Nov 16, R293). Solo measurements keep speed n
 
+The PC watchdog caps each solo claim at 1200 seconds by default. A longer bench sets `PC_GPU_SOLO_MAX_S` for that
+step, and a job with several timed sessions claims the card once per session. Each wrapped step has a ceiling sized to
+its own work and an idle check for console output, new files in its output folder, and CPU time across its tree. The
+idle limit is 300 seconds for Godot and 600 seconds for Blender and other tools. A step can raise it with `TMO_IDLE_S`.
+Known fatal lines and 50 consecutive copies of one error line stop the step immediately. A stop returns 124, keeps the
+child logs, and records the step and reason in `timeouts.log`. The watchdog verifies the tree is gone before releasing
+the GPU claim. GPU admission has its own clock, with a 1800-second default in the agent runners and 3600 in the shared
+ship runner. A Jolt stall in the issue 1078 probe held the GPU solo for almost an hour and queued work timed out behind
+it (Board, Nov 19, issue 1192).
+

# Godot importer would give them (S3TC; the normal map with the normal source, which Godot stores as RGTC). A glb with no lod nodes (round 2's Ab) runs "off"

# generate_lods, the same simplifier the Godot importer uses, and the default threshold of 1 pixel). For LOD on, a single-ship
# distance sweep records the triangles drawn per frame, so the switch distances can be read off, plus stills with LOD on and off
# at four distances for judging pops by eye.

+using System;
+using System.Diagnostics;
+using System.IO;
+using System.Threading;
+public static class FakeGodot {
+  static void StartChild(string exe, string role, string output) {
+    Process.Start(new ProcessStartInfo(exe, role + " \"" + output + "\"") {
+      UseShellExecute = false, CreateNoWindow = true
+    });
+  }
+  public static int Main(string[] args) {
+    string mode = args[0], output = args[1];
+    string exe = typeof(FakeGodot).Assembly.Location;
+    File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(output, "pid-" + mode), Process.GetCurrentProcess().Id + "\n");
+    if (mode == "leaf") { Thread.Sleep(Timeout.Infinite); return 0; }
+    if (mode == "branch") {
+      StartChild(exe, "leaf", output);
+      Thread.Sleep(Timeout.Infinite);
+      return 0;
+    }
+    if (mode == "new-files" || mode == "cpu") {
+      var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
+      int file = 0;
+      while (clock.ElapsedMilliseconds < 1800) {
+        if (mode == "cpu") Thread.SpinWait(10000);
+        else {
+          File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(output, "work-" + file++), "progress");
+          Thread.Sleep(150);
+        }
+      }
+      return 7;
+    }
+    if (mode == "mixed") {
+      for (int i = 0; i < 60; i++) {
+        Console.Error.WriteLine("ERROR: fixture retry");
+        Console.WriteLine("other output");
+      }
+      return 7;
+    }
+    StartChild(exe, "branch", output);
+    if (!SpinWait.SpinUntil(() => File.Exists(Path.Combine(output, "pid-leaf")), 5000)) return 9;
+    string claim = Path.Combine(output, "gpu", "gpu_slot_1");
+    if (mode == "idle" || mode == "solo") {
+      if (!File.Exists(claim) || !File.ReadAllText(claim).Contains("1192")) return 8;
+      Console.WriteLine("fixture saw held GPU claim");
+    }
+    if (mode == "idle") Console.Error.Write("last output before the hang");
+    if (mode == "jolt") Console.Error.Write("Jolt Physics job system exceeded the maximum number of jobs");
+    if (mode == "repeat") for (int i = 0; i < 50; i++) Console.WriteLine("ERROR: fixture retry");
+    if (mode == "solo") {
+      Console.WriteLine("launcher exited while descendants were still running");
+      return 7;
+    }
+    Thread.Sleep(Timeout.Infinite);
+    return 0;
+  }
+}

 jobs and extend things out" (Ryan, Nov 16, R293). Solo measurements keep speed numbers honest. Replaces the two-job cap
 (R276, Nov 15) and the one-job limit before it (R173b, effects-09); the gaming pause (R173) remains. Set: Ryan, Nov 16 (R293).
 
+The PC watchdog caps each solo claim at 1200 seconds by default. A longer bench sets `PC_GPU_SOLO_MAX_S` for that
+step, and a job with several timed sessions claims the card once per session. Each wrapped step has a ceiling sized to
+its own work and an idle check for console output, new files in its output folder, and CPU time across its tree. The
+idle limit is 300 seconds for Godot and 600 seconds for Blender and other tools. A step can raise it with `TMO_IDLE_S`.
+Known fatal lines and 50 consecutive copies of one error line stop the step immediately. A stop returns 124, keeps the
+child logs, and records the step and reason in `timeouts.log`. The watchdog verifies the tree is gone before releasing
+the GPU claim. GPU admission has its own clock, with an 1800-second default in the agent runners and 3600 in the shared
+ship runner. A Jolt stall in the issue 1078 probe held the GPU solo for almost an hour and queued work timed out behind
+it (Board, Nov 19, issue 1192).
+
 3.5 **Every PC queue job is wrapped in the tmo.bat watchdog, ends with exit /b, and carries no |, &, < or > in its
 arguments.** Agent runs stop after STALL_MIN minutes without progress (default 30), or at a hard ceiling (default six
 hours; CODEX_CAP and WORK_CAP override the ceiling in seconds). Progress is a changed file write time in the run repo,

+function Initialize-StepWatchdog {
+  # Start suspended and assign the job before any child code can spawn a process. Windows keeps descendants in
+  # the job even after their parent exits and accounts for CPU used by children that exit between polls.
+  Add-Type -TypeDefinition @'
+using System;
+using System.ComponentModel;
+using System.Diagnostics;
+using System.IO;
+using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
+using System.Text;
+using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
+using System.Threading;
+
+public sealed class RunnerStep : IDisposable {
+  [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
+  struct Startup {
+    public int size;
+    public IntPtr reserved, desktop, title;
+    public int x, y, width, height, xChars, yChars, fill, flags;
+    public short show, reservedSize;
+    public IntPtr reservedBytes, input, output, error;
+  }
+  [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
+  struct ProcessInfo {
+    public IntPtr process, thread;
+    public int pid, tid;
+  }
+  [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
+  public struct Usage {
+    public long UserTime, KernelTime, PeriodUserTime, PeriodKernelTime;
+    public uint PageFaults, Total, Active, Terminated;
+    public long CpuTime { get { return UserTime + KernelTime; } }
+  }
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern IntPtr CreateJobObject(IntPtr attrs, IntPtr name);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool SetInformationJobObject(IntPtr job, int kind, byte[] data, int size);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool QueryInformationJobObject(IntPtr job, int kind, out Usage data, int size, IntPtr written);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool AssignProcessToJobObject(IntPtr job, IntPtr process);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool TerminateJobObject(IntPtr job, uint code);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool TerminateProcess(IntPtr process, uint code);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern uint ResumeThread(IntPtr thread);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool SetHandleInformation(IntPtr handle, uint mask, uint flags);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll")] static extern IntPtr GetStdHandle(int number);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll")] static extern IntPtr GetCurrentProcess();
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool DuplicateHandle(IntPtr source, IntPtr handle, IntPtr target, out IntPtr copy, uint access, bool inherit, uint options);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll")] static extern bool CloseHandle(IntPtr handle);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Unicode, SetLastError = true)]
+  static extern bool CreateProcess(string exe, StringBuilder command, IntPtr processAttrs, IntPtr threadAttrs,
+    bool inherit, uint flags, IntPtr env, string cwd, ref Startup startup, out ProcessInfo info);
+
+  readonly IntPtr job;
+  public Process Root { get; private set; }
+  public RunnerStep(bool gpu) {
+    job = CreateJobObject(IntPtr.Zero, IntPtr.Zero);
+    if (job == IntPtr.Zero) throw new Win32Exception();
+    if (!gpu) return; // A successful ordinary queue launcher may leave background work running.
+    // JOBOBJECT_EXTENDED_LIMIT_INFORMATION, LimitFlags = JOB_OBJECT_LIMIT_KILL_ON_JOB_CLOSE.
+    byte[] limits = new byte[IntPtr.Size == 8 ? 144 : 112];
+    limits[17] = 0x20;
+    if (!SetInformationJobObject(job, 9, limits, limits.Length)) {
+      var error = new Win32Exception();
+      CloseHandle(job);
+      throw error;
+    }
+  }
+  public void Start(string exe, string args, string cwd, string output, string error) {
+    using (var stdout = new FileStream(output, FileMode.CreateNew, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.ReadWrite))
+    using (var stderr = new FileStream(error, FileMode.CreateNew, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.ReadWrite)) {
+      var startup = new Startup { size = Marshal.SizeOf(typeof(Startup)), flags = 0x100 }; // STARTF_USESTDHANDLES
+      startup.output = stdout.SafeFileHandle.DangerousGetHandle();
+      startup.error = stderr.SafeFileHandle.DangerousGetHandle();
+      if (!SetHandleInformation(startup.output, 1, 1) || !SetHandleInformation(startup.error, 1, 1))
+        throw new Win32Exception();
+      // A caller may have no stdin (for example, the queue running under a service).
+      IntPtr self = GetCurrentProcess();
+      DuplicateHandle(self, GetStdHandle(-10), self, out startup.input, 0, true, 2);
+      ProcessInfo info;
+      try {
+        var command = new StringBuilder("\"" + exe + "\" " + args);
+        if (!CreateProcess(exe, command, IntPtr.Zero, IntPtr.Zero, true, 4, IntPtr.Zero, cwd, ref startup, out info))
+          throw new Win32Exception();
+      } finally {
+        if (startup.input != IntPtr.Zero) CloseHandle(startup.input);
+      }
+      bool started = false;
+      try {
+        if (!AssignProcessToJobObject(job, info.process)) throw new Win32Exception();
+        Root = Process.GetProcessById(info.pid);
+        var handle = Root.Handle; // Keep the process identity and its exit code after exit.
+        if (ResumeThread(info.thread) == UInt32.MaxValue) throw new Win32Exception();
+        started = true;
+      } finally {
+        if (!started) TerminateProcess(info.process, 125);
+        CloseHandle(info.thread);
+        CloseHandle(info.process);
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  public Usage ReadUsage() {
+    Usage usage;
+    if (!QueryInformationJobObject(job, 1, out usage, Marshal.SizeOf(typeof(Usage)), IntPtr.Zero))
+      throw new Win32Exception();
+    return usage;
+  }
+  public void Kill() {
+    if (!TerminateJobObject(job, 124)) throw new Win32Exception();
+  }
+  public void Dispose() {
+    if (Root != null) Root.Dispose();
+    CloseHandle(job);
+  }
+}
+
+public sealed class RunnerStepFiles : IDisposable {
+  readonly FileSystemWatcher watcher;
+  readonly string folder;
+  int changes;
+  public RunnerStepFiles(string output) {
+    if (String.IsNullOrEmpty(output)) return;
+    folder = Path.GetFullPath(output).TrimEnd('\\', '/') + Path.DirectorySeparatorChar;
+    string parent = folder;
+    while (!Directory.Exists(parent)) parent = Path.GetDirectoryName(parent.TrimEnd('\\', '/'));
+    watcher = new FileSystemWatcher(parent) { IncludeSubdirectories = true, NotifyFilter = NotifyFilters.FileName };
+    watcher.Created += Changed;
+    watcher.Renamed += Changed;
+    watcher.Error += (sender, e) => Interlocked.Increment(ref changes); // An overflow also means file activity.
+    watcher.EnableRaisingEvents = true;
+  }
+  void Changed(object sender, FileSystemEventArgs e) {
+    if (e.FullPath.StartsWith(folder, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) Interlocked.Increment(ref changes);
+  }
+  public int Changes { get { return Volatile.Read(ref changes); } }
+  public void Dispose() { if (watcher != null) watcher.Dispose(); }
+}
+
+public sealed class RunnerStepOutput : IDisposable {
+  // One shared list for Godot, Blender and other steps. Keep the matched phrase in the log reason.
+  static readonly Regex fatal = new Regex("Jolt Physics job system exceeded|Out of memory|Assertion failed", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
+  static readonly Regex errorLine = new Regex(@"\b(error|fatal|exception|failed|failure|assertion)\b", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
+  readonly FileStream source;
+  readonly Stream target;
+  readonly byte[] bytes = new byte[65536];
+  readonly char[] chars = new char[65536];
+  readonly Decoder decoder = Encoding.UTF8.GetDecoder();
+  readonly StringBuilder line = new StringBuilder();
+  string previousError;
+  int repeats;
+  bool longLine;
+  public string KnownFatal { get; private set; }
+  public bool RepeatedError { get; private set; }
+  public bool Pending { get { return source.Position < source.Length; } }
+  public RunnerStepOutput(string path, bool stderr) {
+    source = new FileStream(path, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read, FileShare.ReadWrite);
+    target = stderr ? Console.OpenStandardError() : Console.OpenStandardOutput();
+  }
+  public void ResetRepeats() { previousError = null; repeats = 0; RepeatedError = false; }
+  public bool Drain(bool scan) {
+    int count = source.Read(bytes, 0, bytes.Length);
+    if (count == 0) return false;
+    target.Write(bytes, 0, count);
+    target.Flush();
+    if (!scan) return true;
+    int length = decoder.GetChars(bytes, 0, count, chars, 0);
+    for (int i = 0; i < length; i++) {
+      if (chars[i] == '\n') {
+        string text = line.ToString().Trim();
+        ScanFatal(text);
+        if (longLine || !errorLine.IsMatch(text)) { previousError = null; repeats = 0; }
+        else {
+          repeats = text == previousError ? repeats + 1 : 1;
+          previousError = text;
+          if (repeats >= 50) RepeatedError = true;
+        }
+        line.Clear();
+        longLine = false;
+      } else if (line.Length == 8192) {
+        ScanFatal(line.ToString());
+        line.Remove(0, 4096);
+        longLine = true;
+        line.Append(chars[i]);
+      } else line.Append(chars[i]);
+    }
+    ScanFatal(line.ToString());
+    return true;
+  }
+  void ScanFatal(string text) {
+    if (KnownFatal != null) return;
+    var match = fatal.Match(text);
+    if (match.Success) KnownFatal = match.Value;
+  }
+  public void Dispose() { source.Dispose(); }
+}
+'@
+}
+function Get-StepLimit($Value, $Default, $Name) {
+  $number = $Default
+  if ($Value -and ($Value -notmatch '^\d+$' -or -not [int]::TryParse($Value, [ref]$number) -or $number -lt 1)) {
+    throw "$Name must be a positive number of seconds"
+  }
+  return $number
+}
+function Stop-StepTree($Job, $Log, $Step) {
+  # Keep the claim while Windows still reports active processes, including during cleanup after a watchdog error.
+  while (($usage = $Job.ReadUsage()).Active) {
+    try { $Job.Kill() }
+    catch { Write-KillLog $Log "$Step kill failed, retrying with GPU claim held: $($_.Exception.Message)" }
+    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 50
+  }
+  Write-KillLog $Log "$Step kill verified: all $($usage.Total) processes gone (root PID $($Job.Root.Id))"
+}

from pathlib import Path

for name, steps in stages.items():
    file = Path(\"tools/recipe\") / name
    lines = file.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\").splitlines()
    for mark, label in steps:
        matches = [i for i, line in enumerate(lines) if line.startswith('\"%BPY%\" ') and mark in line]

        lines.insert(i, f'set \"TMO_STEP=recipe {name[0]} {label}\"')
    file.write_bytes((\"\\r\\n\".join(lines) + \"\\r\\n\").encode(\"utf-8\"))
adapter = Path(\"tools/recipe/install_godot.bat\")

+The PC watchdog caps each solo claim at 1200 seconds by default. A longer bench sets `PC_GPU_SOLO_MAX_S` for that
+step, and a job with several timed sessions claims the card once per session. Each wrapped step has a ceiling sized to
+its own work and an idle check for console output, new files in its output folder, and CPU time across its tree. The
+idle limit is 300 seconds for Godot and 600 seconds for Blender and other tools. A step can raise it with `TMO_IDLE_S`.
+Known fatal lines and 50 consecutive copies of one error line stop the step immediately. A stop returns 124, keeps the
+child logs, and records the step and reason in `timeouts.log`. The watchdog verifies the tree is gone before releasing
+the GPU claim. GPU admission has its own clock, with an 1800-second default in the agent runners and 3600 in the shared
+ship runner. A Jolt stall in the issue 1078 probe held the GPU solo for almost an hour and queued work timed out behind
+it (Board, Nov 19, issue 1192).
+

 hours; CODEX_CAP and WORK_CAP override the ceiling in seconds). Progress is a changed file write time in the run repo,

 function Write-KillLog($Log, $Line) {
   Write-Output "run_timeout: $Line"
-  try { "$(Get-Date -Format s) $Line" | Out-File -LiteralPath $Log -Append -Encoding utf8 }
-  catch { Write-Output "run_timeout: could not append kill log: $($_.Exception.Message)" }
+  for ($attempt = 1; ; $attempt++) {
+    try { [IO.File]::AppendAllText($Log, "$(Get-Date -Format s) $Line`r`n"); return }
+    catch [IO.IOException] {
+      # Several watchdogs can report to the same queue log.
+      if ($attempt -ge 10) { Write-Output "run_timeout: could not append kill log: $($_.Exception.Message)"; return }
+      Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 25
+    }
+    catch { Write-Output "run_timeout: could not append kill log: $($_.Exception.Message)"; return }
+  }

+function Initialize-StepWatchdog {
+  # Start suspended and assign the job before any child code can spawn a process. Windows keeps descendants in
+  # the job even after their parent exits and accounts for CPU used by children that exit between polls.
+  Add-Type -TypeDefinition @'
+using System;
+using System.ComponentModel;
+using System.Diagnostics;
+using System.IO;
+using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
+using System.Text;
+using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
+using System.Threading;
+
+public sealed class RunnerStep : IDisposable {
+  [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
+  struct Startup {
+    public int size;
+    public IntPtr reserved, desktop, title;
+    public int x, y, width, height, xChars, yChars, fill, flags;
+    public short show, reservedSize;
+    public IntPtr reservedBytes, input, output, error;
+  }
+  [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
+  struct ProcessInfo {
+    public IntPtr process, thread;
+    public int pid, tid;
+  }
+  [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)]
+  public struct Usage {
+    public long UserTime, KernelTime, PeriodUserTime, PeriodKernelTime;
+    public uint PageFaults, Total, Active, Terminated;
+    public long CpuTime { get { return UserTime + KernelTime; } }
+  }
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern IntPtr CreateJobObject(IntPtr attrs, IntPtr name);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool SetInformationJobObject(IntPtr job, int kind, byte[] data, int size);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool QueryInformationJobObject(IntPtr job, int kind, out Usage data, int size, IntPtr written);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool AssignProcessToJobObject(IntPtr job, IntPtr process);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool TerminateJobObject(IntPtr job, uint code);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool TerminateProcess(IntPtr process, uint code);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern uint ResumeThread(IntPtr thread);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool SetHandleInformation(IntPtr handle, uint mask, uint flags);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll")] static extern IntPtr GetStdHandle(int number);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll")] static extern IntPtr GetCurrentProcess();
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError = true)] static extern bool DuplicateHandle(IntPtr source, IntPtr handle, IntPtr target, out IntPtr copy, uint access, bool inherit, uint options);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll")] static extern bool CloseHandle(IntPtr handle);
+  [DllImport("kernel32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Unicode, SetLastError = true)]
+  static extern bool CreateProcess(string exe, StringBuilder command, IntPtr processAttrs, IntPtr threadAttrs,
+    bool inherit, uint flags, IntPtr env, string cwd, ref Startup startup, out ProcessInfo info);
+
+  readonly IntPtr job;
+  public Process Root { get; private set; }
+  public RunnerStep(bool gpu) {
+    job = CreateJobObject(IntPtr.Zero, IntPtr.Zero);
+    if (job == IntPtr.Zero) throw new Win32Exception();
+    if (!gpu) return; // A successful ordinary queue launcher may leave background work running.
+    // JOBOBJECT_EXTENDED_LIMIT_INFORMATION, LimitFlags = JOB_OBJECT_LIMIT_KILL_ON_JOB_CLOSE.
+    byte[] limits = new byte[IntPtr.Size == 8 ? 144 : 112];
+    limits[17] = 0x20;
+    if (!SetInformationJobObject(job, 9, limits, limits.Length)) {
+      var error = new Win32Exception();
+      CloseHandle(job);
+      throw error;
+    }
+  }
+  public void Start(string exe, string args, string cwd, string output, string error) {
+    using (var stdout = new FileStream(output, FileMode.CreateNew, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.ReadWrite))
+    using (var stderr = new FileStream(error, FileMode.CreateNew, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.ReadWrite)) {
+      var startup = new Startup { size = Marshal.SizeOf(typeof(Startup)), flags = 0x100 }; // STARTF_USESTDHANDLES
+      startup.output = stdout.SafeFileHandle.DangerousGetHandle();
+      startup.error = stderr.SafeFileHandle.DangerousGetHandle();
+      if (!SetHandleInformation(startup.output, 1, 1) || !SetHandleInformation(startup.error, 1, 1))
+        throw new Win32Exception();
+      // A caller may have no stdin (for example, the queue running under a service).
+      IntPtr self = GetCurrentProcess();
+      DuplicateHandle(self, GetStdHandle(-10), self, out startup.input, 0, true, 2);
+      ProcessInfo info;
+      try {
+        var command = new StringBuilder("\"" + exe + "\" " + args);
+        if (!CreateProcess(exe, command, IntPtr.Zero, IntPtr.Zero, true, 4, IntPtr.Zero, cwd, ref startup, out info))
+          throw new Win32Exception();
+      } finally {
+        if (startup.input != IntPtr.Zero) CloseHandle(startup.input);
+      }
+      bool started = false;
+      try {
+        if (!AssignProcessToJobObject(job, info.process)) throw new Win32Exception();
+        Root = Process.GetProcessById(info.pid);
+        var handle = Root.Handle; // Keep the process identity and its exit code after exit.
+        if (ResumeThread(info.thread) == UInt32.MaxValue) throw new Win32Exception();
+        started = true;
+      } finally {
+        if (!started) TerminateProcess(info.process, 125);
+        CloseHandle(info.thread);
+        CloseHandle(info.process);
+      }
+    }
+  }
+  public Usage ReadUsage() {
+    Usage usage;
+    if (!QueryInformationJobObject(job, 1, out usage, Marshal.SizeOf(typeof(Usage)), IntPtr.Zero))
+      throw new Win32Exception();
+    return usage;
+  }
+  public void Kill() {
+    if (!TerminateJobObject(job, 124)) throw new Win32Exception();
+  }
+  public void Dispose() {
+    if (Root != null) Root.Dispose();
+    CloseHandle(job);
+  }
+}
+
+public sealed class RunnerStepFiles : IDisposable {
+  readonly FileSystemWatcher watcher;
+  readonly string folder;
+  int changes;
+  public RunnerStepFiles(string output) {
+    if (String.IsNullOrEmpty(output)) return;
+    folder = Path.GetFullPath(output).TrimEnd('\\', '/') + Path.DirectorySeparatorChar;
+    string parent = folder;
+    while (!Directory.Exists(parent)) parent = Path.GetDirectoryName(parent.TrimEnd('\\', '/'));
+    watcher = new FileSystemWatcher(parent) { IncludeSubdirectories = true, NotifyFilter = NotifyFilters.FileName };
+    watcher.Created += Changed;
+    watcher.Renamed += Changed;
+    watcher.Error += (sender, e) => Interlocked.Increment(ref changes); // An overflow also means file activity.
+    watcher.EnableRaisingEvents = true;
+  }
+  void Changed(object sender, FileSystemEventArgs e) {
+    if (e.FullPath.StartsWith(folder, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) Interlocked.Increment(ref changes);
+  }
+  public int Changes { get { return Volatile.Read(ref changes); } }
+  public void Dispose() { if (watcher != null) watcher.Dispose(); }
+}
+
+public sealed class RunnerStepOutput : IDisposable {
+  // One shared list for Godot, Blender and other steps. Keep the matched phrase in the log reason.
+  static readonly Regex fatal = new Regex("Jolt Physics job system exceeded|Out of memory|Assertion failed", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
+  static readonly Regex errorLine = new Regex(@"\b(error|fatal|exception|failed|failure|assertion)\b", RegexOptions.IgnoreCase);
+  readonly FileStream source;
+  readonly Stream target;
+  readonly byte[] bytes = new byte[65536];
+  readonly char[] chars = new char[65536];
+  readonly Decoder decoder = Encoding.UTF8.GetDecoder();
+  readonly StringBuilder line = new StringBuilder();
+  string previousError;
+  int repeats;
+  bool longLine;
+  public string KnownFatal { get; private set; }
+  public bool RepeatedError { get; private set; }
+  public bool Pending { get { return source.Position < source.Length; } }
+  public RunnerStepOutput(string path, bool stderr) {
+    source = new FileStream(path, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read, FileShare.ReadWrite);
+    target = stderr ? Console.OpenStandardError() : Console.OpenStandardOutput();
+  }
+  public void ResetRepeats() { previousError = null; repeats = 0; RepeatedError = false; }
+  public bool Drain(bool scan) {
+    int count = source.Read(bytes, 0, bytes.Length);
+    if (count == 0) return false;
+    target.Write(bytes, 0, count);
+    target.Flush();
+    if (!scan) return true;
+    int length = decoder.GetChars(bytes, 0, count, chars, 0);
+    for (int i = 0; i < length; i++) {
+      if (chars[i] == '\n') {
+        string text = line.ToString().Trim();
+        ScanFatal(text);
+        if (longLine || !errorLine.IsMatch(text)) { previousError = null; repeats = 0; }
+        else {
+          repeats = text == previousError ? repeats + 1 : 1;
+          previousError = text;
+          if (repeats >= 50) RepeatedError = true;
+        }
+        line.Clear();
+        longLine = false;
+      } else if (line.Length == 8192) {
+        ScanFatal(line.ToString());
+        line.Remove(0, 4096);
+        longLine = true;
+        line.Append(chars[i]);
+      } else line.Append(chars[i]);
+    }
+    ScanFatal(line.ToString());
+    return true;
+  }
+  void ScanFatal(string text) {
+    if (KnownFatal != null) return;
+    var match = fatal.Match(text);
+    if (match.Success) KnownFatal = match.Value;
+  }
+  public void Dispose() { source.Dispose(); }
+}
+'@
+}
+function Get-StepLimit($Value, $Default, $Name) {
+  $number = $Default
+  if ($Value -and ($Value -notmatch '^\d+$' -or -not [int]::TryParse($Value, [ref]$number) -or $number -lt 1)) {
+    throw "$Name must be a positive number of seconds"
+  }
+  return $number
+}
+function Stop-StepTree($Job, $Log, $Step) {
+  # Keep the claim while Windows still reports active processes, including during cleanup after a watchdog error.
+  while (($usage = $Job.ReadUsage()).Active) {
+    try { $Job.Kill() }
+    catch { Write-KillLog $Log "$Step kill failed, retrying with GPU claim held: $($_.Exception.Message)" }
+    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 50
+  }
+  Write-KillLog $Log "$Step kill verified: all $($usage.Total) processes gone (root PID $($Job.Root.Id))"
+}

+// A fake Godot executable uses Windows processes and the real claim helper. Its GPU go file and claims live in TEMP.

+const gameSource = path.join(root, 'FakeGodot.cs');
+fs.writeFileSync(gameSource, String.raw`
+using System;
+using System.Diagnostics;
+using System.IO;
+using System.Threading;
+public static class FakeGodot {
+  static void StartChild(string exe, string role, string output) {
+    Process.Start(new ProcessStartInfo(exe, role + " \"" + output + "\"") {
+      UseShellExecute = false, CreateNoWindow = true
+    });
+  }
+  public static int Main(string[] args) {
+    string mode = args[0], output = args[1];
+    string exe = typeof(FakeGodot).Assembly.Location;
+    File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(output, "pid-" + mode), Process.GetCurrentProcess().Id + "\n");
+    if (mode == "leaf") { Thread.Sleep(Timeout.Infinite); return 0; }
+    if (mode == "branch") {
+      StartChild(exe, "leaf", output);
+      Thread.Sleep(Timeout.Infinite);
+      return 0;
+    }
+    if (mode == "new-files" || mode == "cpu") {
+      var clock = Stopwatch.StartNew();
+      int file = 0;
+      while (clock.ElapsedMilliseconds < 1800) {
+        if (mode == "cpu") Thread.SpinWait(10000);
+        else {
+          File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(output, "work-" + file++), "progress");
+          Thread.Sleep(150);
+        }
+      }
+      return 7;
+    }
+    if (mode == "mixed") {
+      for (int i = 0; i < 60; i++) {
+        Console.Error.WriteLine("ERROR: fixture retry");
+        Console.WriteLine("other output");
+      }
+      return 7;
+    }
+    StartChild(exe, "branch", output);
+    if (!SpinWait.SpinUntil(() => File.Exists(Path.Combine(output, "pid-leaf")), 5000)) return 9;
+    string claim = Path.Combine(output, "gpu", "gpu_slot_1");
+    if (mode == "idle" || mode == "solo") {
+      if (!File.Exists(claim) || !File.ReadAllText(claim).Contains("1192")) return 8;
+      Console.WriteLine("fixture saw held GPU claim");
+    }
+    if (mode == "idle") Console.Error.Write("last output before the hang");
+    if (mode == "jolt") Console.Error.Write("Jolt Physics job system exceeded the maximum number of jobs");
+    if (mode == "repeat") for (int i = 0; i < 50; i++) Console.WriteLine("ERROR: fixture retry");
+    if (mode == "solo") {
+      Console.WriteLine("launcher exited while descendants were still running");
+      return 7;
+    }
+    Thread.Sleep(Timeout.Infinite);
+    return 0;
+  }
+}
+`);
+const compile = path.join(root, 'fake-godot.ps1');

+});
+assert.equal(built.status, 0, built.stdout + built.stderr + String(built.error || ''));
+const tmo = path.join(pc, 'tmo.bat');
+for (const [mode, reason] of [
+  ['idle', 'IDLE after 1 s'], ['jolt', 'FATAL Jolt Physics job system exceeded'],

+      PC_GPU_LANE: '', PC_GPU_ISSUE: '1192', PC_GPU_WAIT_SECONDS: '5', PC_GPU_MODE: '', PC_GPU_WEIGHT: '',
